**[02:44:22.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003817)  
Ay, unahan na.

**[02:44:24.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003818)  
No, but honestly,

**[02:44:26.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003819)  
medyo yun lang po

**[02:44:28.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003820)  
yung gusto namin ipaabot po sa inyo.

**[02:44:30.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003821)  
We apologize.

**[02:44:32.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003822)  
On behalf of CPV,

**[02:44:34.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003823)  
RD, and the APRO committee,

**[02:44:36.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003824)  
we apologize. But

**[02:44:38.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003825)  
hindi po talaga sa amin nakarating

**[02:44:40.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003826)  
yung questions. So it's hard naman po

**[02:44:42.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003827)  
magbasa ng questions na hindi po nakarating

**[02:44:44.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003828)  
po sa amin.

**[02:44:45.800] John Castillo** (asr-003828-split-2)  
Thank you very much,

**[02:44:46.910] John Castillo** (asr-003829)  
Vice Chair Arnie Fuentebella.

**[02:44:48.910] John Castillo** (asr-003830)  
And we, just

**[02:44:50.910] John Castillo** (asr-003831)  
like that, we have reached our

**[02:44:52.910] John Castillo** (asr-003832)  
final segment of

**[02:44:54.910] John Castillo** (asr-003833)  
today's People's Budget Review.

**[02:44:56.910] John Castillo** (asr-003834)  
But to give his

**[02:44:58.910] John Castillo** (asr-003835)  
closing remarks.

**[02:45:00.000] John Castillo** (asr-003836)  
may we call on the Deputy Secretary-General of the Congressional Policy and Budget Research Department,

**[02:45:08.040] John Castillo** (asr-003837)  
Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral.

**[02:45:11.680] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003838)  
Good afternoon to all of us.

**[02:45:16.780] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003839)  
First of all, we would like to express our deepest appreciation and gratitude

**[02:45:21.940] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003840)  
to our legislators present here, led by Senior Vice Chair Garcia

**[02:45:28.440] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003841)  
and the other vice chairpersons of the Committee on Appropriation,

**[02:45:33.800] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003842)  
Congresswoman Garcia, Congressman Arrogancia, Congresswoman Lagdameo,

**[02:45:42.160] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003843)  
Congressman Fuentebella, Congresswoman Bondoc, and Congresswoman Suansing.

**[02:45:49.860] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003844)  
Thank you so much.

**[02:45:50.780] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003845)  
And to all our CSO representatives here present, again, marami pong salamat.

**[02:45:56.440] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003846)  
As we conclude...

**[02:45:58.440] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003847)  
As we conclude today's People's Budget Review, allow me to begin with a very familiar story.

**[02:46:05.500] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003848)  
The story of the blind man and the elephant.

**[02:46:09.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003849)  
Each man touched a different part of the elephant.

**[02:46:13.580] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003850)  
One touched the trunk and said the elephant was like a snake.

**[02:46:19.460] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003851)  
Another touched the leg and said it was like a tree.

**[02:46:23.300] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003852)  
Another touched the side and said it was like a wall.

**[02:46:28.440] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003853)  
It was describing something real, but none, by himself, had the complete picture.

**[02:46:36.920] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003854)  
In many ways, this is also true of the national budget.

**[02:46:41.720] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003855)  
Government agencies see it from the perspective of programs and implementation.

**[02:46:48.780] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003856)  
Economic managers see it from the perspective of fiscal discipline and national priorities.

**[02:46:55.620] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003857)  
Congress sees it through its constitutional power of the purse

**[02:47:01.280] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003866)  
and its responsibility to represent the people.

**[02:47:05.880] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003875)  
Researchers examine the numbers and evidence

**[02:47:09.380] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003882)  
and CSOs and people's organizations see how government policies and programs are actually

**[02:47:17.940] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003883)  
experienced by communities.

**[02:47:21.200] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003884)  
Each perspective is important, but no single perspective gives us the whole picture.

**[02:47:28.660] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003885)  
This is why evidence-based budgeting is so important.

**[02:47:34.020] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003886)  
When Congress decides how trillions of pesos of public resources should be allocated, we

**[02:47:40.440] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003887)  
should be able to answer some very basic questions.

**[02:47:44.840] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003888)  
What problems are we trying to solve?

**[02:47:48.820] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003889)  
What has the government already spent on?

**[02:47:52.300] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003890)  
What has the spending produced?

**[02:47:54.800] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003891)  
Is the program working?

**[02:47:57.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003892)  
Who has benefited?

**[02:47:58.900] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003893)  
And what should we do differently?

**[02:48:01.460] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003894)  
Unfortunately, the evidence needed to

**[02:48:04.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003895)  
answer these questions is often fragmented across agencies and information systems.

**[02:48:11.460] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003896)  
We have financial information on how much has been obligated and disbursed, but information

**[02:48:17.980] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003897)  
on what government actually delivered and what outcomes those expenditures produced

**[02:48:23.740] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003898)  
is not always as readily available, timely, or comparable.

**[02:48:30.160] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003899)  
This points to the need for a more systematic approach to evidence gathering

**[02:48:35.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003900)  
through a unified national monitoring

**[02:48:39.140] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003901)  
and evaluation system.

**[02:48:41.620] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003902)  
Such a system should identify a core set of critical performance indicators that every

**[02:48:48.380] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003903)  
government agency is required to regularly report and publish.

**[02:48:54.640] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003904)  
These indicators should allow us to follow the entire results chain, from inputs and

**[02:49:01.420] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003905)  
expenditures to activities and outputs

**[02:49:05.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003907)  
and ultimately to outcomes and impacts.

**[02:49:09.380] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003908)  
And this information should not remain scattered among individual agencies or buried in periodic

**[02:49:17.380] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003909)  
reports.

**[02:49:18.860] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003910)  
We need an integrated data system that allows Congress, government decision makers, CSOs,

**[02:49:27.520] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003911)  
researchers, and ultimately the public to have real-time or near-real-time

**[02:49:32.780] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003912)  
access to financial and performance information, especially given this age of

**[02:49:39.780] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003913)  
information technology.

**[02:49:42.380] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003914)  
Imagine how this change could change budget deliberation.

**[02:49:47.560] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003915)  
Instead of asking how much did we appropriate or how much was spent, Congress could ask

**[02:49:54.440] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003916)  
what did we accomplish and what we spent.

**[02:49:59.100] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003917)  
This is the kind of evidence base we need for better budgeting.

**[02:50:03.340] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003918)  
But even the best government information system cannot tell us everything.

**[02:50:08.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003919)  
Official indicators tell us what numbers show.

**[02:50:12.580] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003920)  
Citizens and civil society can help tell us what those numbers mean on the ground.

**[02:50:17.980] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003921)  
This is where CSOs and people's organizations become indispensable.

**[02:50:23.500] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003922)  
For legislators, civil society provides an important independent source of information

**[02:50:28.400] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003923)  
and evidence.

**[02:50:31.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003924)  
CSOs can tell us whether services actually reach intended beneficiaries, whether a program

**[02:50:38.020] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003925)  
that appears successful in an agency report is working in communities, and whether important

**[02:50:45.040] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003926)  
needs have been overlooked.

**[02:50:47.520] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003927)  
They help Congress see another part of the elephant.

**[02:50:52.420] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003928)  
This is why the House of Representatives has taken steps to widen people's participation

**[02:50:58.400] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003929)  
in the budget.

**[02:50:59.060] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003930)  
We have established mechanisms for CSO accreditation and participation in agency budget hearings,

**[02:51:08.080] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003931)  
provided opportunities for the submission of position papers and recommendations, undertaken

**[02:51:14.140] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003932)  
budget literacy activities, expanded access to budget information, and created opportunities

**[02:51:21.840] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003933)  
such as today's dialogue for civil society to engage directly with legislators.

**[02:51:29.060] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003934)  
But we must also recognize that participation remains a work in progress.

**[02:51:35.940] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003935)  
Are we hearing from a sufficiently broad range of organizations, including those outside

**[02:51:43.220] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003936)  
Metro Manila and those representing marginalized communities? Do CSOs have the technical capacity

**[02:51:50.520] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003937)  
and information needed to participate effectively?

**[02:51:53.940] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003938)  
Are budget documents provided early enough and in forms that can be readily

**[02:51:59.060] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003939)  
analyzed?

**[02:52:01.820] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003940)  
And perhaps the most important question is whether participation actually influences

**[02:52:07.540] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003941)  
decisions.

**[02:52:08.540] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003942)  
Moving forward, therefore, I believe we need to pursue two mutually reinforcing reforms.

**[02:52:17.300] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003943)  
First, we need a stronger evidence infrastructure, a unified monitoring and evaluation system,

**[02:52:25.160] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003944)  
common and critical performance indicators.

**[02:52:28.360] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003945)  
Interoperable information systems and real-time or near real-time access to financial and

**[02:52:35.680] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003946)  
performance data.

**[02:52:37.320] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003947)  
Second, we need stronger participatory institutions, earlier engagements of CSOs, better access

**[02:52:45.480] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003948)  
to information, stronger budget literacy, clearer channels between CSOs and legislators,

**[02:52:53.640] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003949)  
and mechanisms for tracking how civil society recommendations are considered in the budget process.

**[02:53:01.380] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003952)  
Better data without participation can give us numbers without sufficient context.

**[02:53:08.800] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003953)  
Participation with good data can give us perspectives without sufficient evidence. We need both.

**[02:53:17.240] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003954)  
And this brings us back to the blind man and the elephant.

**[02:53:20.700] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003955)  
The objective is not merely to bring more people around the elephant.

**[02:53:26.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003959)  
It is to ensure that it brings evidence from the part that he or she can see.

**[02:53:34.560] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003963)  
And that's what we put all of that together to put the evidence into an understandable whole.

**[02:53:42.340] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003964)  
That, I believe, is what a truly evidence-based and participatory budget process should accomplish.

**[02:53:48.940] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003965)  
Thus, on behalf of the Congressional Policy and Budget Research Department,

**[02:53:53.900] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003966)  
I thank our members of Congress, our CSOs, PO partners, and everyone who participated in today's dialogue.

**[02:54:03.800] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003967)  
May what we heard today find its way into our continuing budget deliberations,

**[02:54:10.020] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003968)  
our oversight work, and our efforts to further institutionalize meaningful participation in Congress.

**[02:54:18.000] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003969)  
Because ultimately, a people's budget should not only be a budget for the people,

**[02:54:23.900] CPBRD Deputy Secretary General Dr. Romulo Emmanuel Miral Jr.** (asr-003970)  
it should be a budget informed by evidence, accountable for results, and increasingly shaped with the people. Maraming salamat po at magandang hapon sa ating lahat.

**[02:54:40.860] John Castillo** (asr-003971)  
Thank you very much, Dr. Miral.

**[02:54:43.300] John Castillo** (asr-003971-split-2)  
Thank you very much, Dr. Miral.

**[02:54:45.720] John Castillo** (asr-003972)  
So, with that, the People's Budget Review Second Cycle is closed.

**[02:54:52.320] John Castillo** (asr-003973)  
And on behalf of the House of Representatives and the CPBRD,

**[02:54:55.880] John Castillo** (asr-003974)  
we would like to thank you, our civil society partners, our House members,

**[02:55:00.420] John Castillo** (asr-003975)  
and everyone who participated in today's discussion.

**[02:55:04.420] John Castillo** (asr-003976)  
Thank you for bringing your ideas, and thank you for each...
